\begin{split}\nabla_{\mathbf{C}_{i}}\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}&=\nabla_{\mathbf{A}_{i}}\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{C}_{i}\mathbf{D}_{i,11}^{\top}+\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{D}_{i,12}^{\top})+\nabla_{\mathbf{A}_{i}}\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}^{\top}(\mathbf{C}_{i}\mathbf{D}_{i,11}+\mathbf{R}_{i}\mathbf{D}_{i,21}),\\
\nabla_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}&=\nabla_{\mathbf{A}_{i}}\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}[\mathbf{C}_{i}~\mathbf{P}_{i}][\mathbf{D}_{i,21}~\mathbf{D}_{i,22}]^{\top},\\
\nabla_{\mathbf{P}_{i}}\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}&=\nabla_{\mathbf{A}_{i}}\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}^{\top}[\mathbf{C}_{i}~\mathbf{R}_{i}][\mathbf{D}_{i,12}^{\top}~\mathbf{D}_{i,22}^{\top}]^{\top},\\
\text{and }\nabla_{\mathbf{D}_{i}}\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}&=[\mathbf{C}_{i}~\mathbf{R}_{i}]^{\top}\nabla_{\mathbf{A}_{i}}\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}[\mathbf{C}_{i}~\mathbf{P}_{i}].\end{split}

(35)

Denote the following block structure of
\mathbf{D}_{i}
as

\mathbf{D}_{i}=\begin{pmatrix}\mathbf{D}_{i,11}&\mathbf{D}_{i,12}\\
\mathbf{D}_{i,21}&\mathbf{D}_{i,22}\end{pmatrix},

where
\mathbf{D}_{i,11}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{i}\times d_{i}},\mathbf{D}_{i,12}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{i}\times(r_{i}-d_{i})},\mathbf{D}_{i,21}\in\mathbb{R}^{(r_{i}-d_{i})\times d_{i}},\mathbf{D}_{i,22}\in\mathbb{R}^{(r_{i}-d_{i})\times(r_{i}-d_{i})}
are block matrices in
\mathbf{D}_{i}
, for
i=1,2
.
Finally, combined with the partial gradients of the regularization terms, the partial gradients of
\overline{\mathcal{L}}
are given by:

\displaystyle\nabla_{\mathbf{C}_{i}}\overline{\mathcal{L}}=

\displaystyle\nabla_{\mathbf{A}_{i}}\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{C}_{i}\mathbf{D}_{i,11}^{\top}+\mathbf{P}_{i}\mathbf{D}_{i,12}^{\top})+\nabla_{\mathbf{A}_{i}}\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}^{\top}(\mathbf{C}_{i}\mathbf{D}_{i,11}+\mathbf{R}_{i}\mathbf{D}_{i,21})

(36)

\displaystyle+(-1)^{i+1}\lambda_{1}\left(\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)\left(\mathbf{A}_{i}[\mathbf{C}_{i}~\mathbf{P}_{i}][\mathbf{D}_{i,11}~\mathbf{D}_{i,12}]^{\top}+\mathbf{A}_{i}^{\top}[\mathbf{C}_{i}~\mathbf{R}_{i}][\mathbf{D}_{i,11}^{\top}~\mathbf{D}_{i,21}^{\top}]^{\top}\right)

\displaystyle+\lambda_{2}\left(2\mathbf{C}_{i}(\mathbf{C}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{C}_{i}-b^{2}\mathbf{I}_{d_{i}})+\mathbf{R_{i}}\mathbf{R}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{C}_{i}+\mathbf{P_{i}}\mathbf{P}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{C}_{i}\right),

\displaystyle\nabla_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}\overline{\mathcal{L}}=

\displaystyle\nabla_{\mathbf{A}_{i}}\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}[\mathbf{C}_{i}~\mathbf{P}_{i}][\mathbf{D}_{i,21}\;\mathbf{D}_{i,22}]^{\top}+(-1)^{i+1}\lambda_{1}\left(\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)\mathbf{A}_{i}[\mathbf{C}_{i}~\mathbf{P}_{i}][\mathbf{D}_{i,21}~\mathbf{D}_{i,22}]^{\top}

\displaystyle+\lambda_{2}\left(\mathbf{R}_{i}(\mathbf{R}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{R}_{i}-b^{2}\mathbf{I}_{r_{i}-d_{i}})+\mathbf{C}_{i}\mathbf{C}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{R}_{i}\right),

\displaystyle\nabla_{\mathbf{P}_{i}}\overline{\mathcal{L}}=

\displaystyle\nabla_{\mathbf{A}_{i}}\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}^{\top}[\mathbf{C}_{i}\;\mathbf{R}_{i}][\mathbf{D}_{i,12}^{\top}\;\mathbf{D}_{i,22}^{\top}]^{\top}+(-1)^{i+1}\lambda_{1}\left(\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)\mathbf{A}_{i}^{\top}[\mathbf{C}_{i}~\mathbf{R}_{i}][\mathbf{D}_{i,12}^{\top}~\mathbf{D}_{i,22}^{\top}]^{\top}

\displaystyle+\lambda_{2}\left(\mathbf{P}_{i}(\mathbf{P}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{P}_{i}-b^{2}\mathbf{I}_{r_{i}-d_{i}})+\mathbf{C_{i}}\mathbf{C}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{P}_{i}\right),

\displaystyle\nabla_{\mathbf{D}_{i}}\overline{\mathcal{L}}=

\displaystyle[\mathbf{C}_{i}\;\mathbf{R}_{i}]^{\top}\nabla_{\mathbf{A}_{i}}\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}[\mathbf{C}_{i}\;\mathbf{P}_{i}]+(-1)^{i+1}\lambda_{1}\left(\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)[\mathbf{C}_{i}~\mathbf{R}_{i}]^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{i}[\mathbf{C}_{i}~\mathbf{P}_{i}].

Appendix C
Computational Convergence Analysis

In this appendix, we present the proof of Theorem
1
, i.e., the computational convergence of regularized gradient decent algorithm proposed in the main article. To make it easy to read, it is divided into several steps. Auxiliary lemmas and their proofs are presented in Appendix
C.2
.

C.1
Proof of Theorem
1

Step 1.

(Notations, conditions, and proof outline)

We begin by introducing some important notations and conditions required for the convergence analysis. Other notations not mentioned in this step are inherited from Appendix
B
. We then provide an outline of the proof, highlighting the key intermediate results and main ideas.

To begin, we restate the definitions of the measures quantifying the estimation error and the statistical error. Throughout this article, the true values of all parameters are defined by letters with an asterisk superscript, i.e.,
\{\mathbf{C}_{i}^{*},\mathbf{R}_{i}^{*},\mathbf{P}_{i}^{*},\mathbf{D}_{i}^{*},\mathbf{A}_{i}^{*}\}_{i=1}^{2}
. Let
\mathbb{O}^{n_{1}\times n_{2}}
be the set of
n_{1}\times n_{2}
matrices with orthonormal columns. When
n_{1}=n_{2}=n
, we use
\mathbb{O}^{n}
for short. For the
j
-th iterate, we quantify the combined estiamtion errors up to optimal rotations defined in (
13
) as

\begin{split}\mathrm{dist}(\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{(j)},\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{*})^{2}=\min_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{O}_{i,r},\mathbf{O}_{i,p}\in\mathbb{O}^{r_{i}-d_{i}}\\
\mathbf{O}_{i,c}\in\mathbb{O}^{d_{i}}\end{subarray}}\sum_{i=1,2}\Big\{&\|\mathbf{C}_{i}^{(j)}-\mathbf{C}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{i,c}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}+\|\mathbf{R}_{i}^{(j)}-\mathbf{R}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{i,r}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\\
+\|\mathbf{P}_{i}^{(j)}-\mathbf{P}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{i,p}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}+\|&\mathbf{D}_{i}^{(j)}-\text{diag}(\mathbf{O}_{i,c},\mathbf{O}_{i,r})^{\top}\mathbf{D}_{i}^{*}\text{diag}(\mathbf{O}_{i,c},\mathbf{O}_{i,p})\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\Big\},\end{split}

(37)

and the corresponding optimal rotations as
\mathbf{O}_{i,c}^{(j)},\mathbf{O}_{i,r}^{(j)},\mathbf{O}_{i,p}^{(j)}
, for
i=1,2
. For simplicity, we let
\mathbf{O}_{i,u}:=\text{diag}(\mathbf{O}_{i,c},\mathbf{O}_{i,r})
,
\mathbf{O}_{i,v}:=\text{diag}(\mathbf{O}_{i,c},\mathbf{O}_{i,p})
, and use
\mathrm{dist}^{2}_{(j)}
to represent
\mathrm{dist}(\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{(j)},\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{*})^{2}
.

In addition, as defined in Definition
2
, the statistical error is quantified by

\xi(r_{1},r_{2},d_{1},d_{2}):=\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}[\mathbf{C}_{i}~\mathbf{R}_{i}]\in\mathbb{O}^{p_{i}\times r_{i}},\\
[\mathbf{C}_{i}~\mathbf{P}_{i}]\in\mathbb{O}^{p_{i}\times r_{i}},\\
\mathbf{D}_{i}\in\mathbb{R}^{r_{i}},\left\|\mathbf{D}_{i}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}=1\end{subarray}}\left\langle\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*}),[\mathbf{C}_{2}~\mathbf{R}_{2}]\mathbf{D}_{2}[\mathbf{C}_{2}~\mathbf{P}_{2}]^{\top}\otimes[\mathbf{C}_{1}~\mathbf{R}_{1}]\mathbf{D}_{1}[\mathbf{C}_{1}~\mathbf{P}_{1}]^{\top}\right\rangle,

(38)

where
\mathbf{A}^{*}=\mathbf{A}_{2}^{*}\otimes\mathbf{A}_{1}^{*}
and
\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A})
is referred to as the least-squares loss function with respect to the identifiable Kronecker product type parameter
\mathbf{A}=\mathbf{A}_{2}\otimes\mathbf{A}_{1}
.

Next, we state three conditions required for convergence analysis. The first condition stipulates that
\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A})
satisfies the RSC/RSS condition defined in
1
. Specifically, we assume there exist constants
0<\alpha\leq\beta
such that for any pair of identifiable parameter matrices
\mathbf{A}=\mathbf{A}_{2}\otimes\mathbf{A}_{1}
and
\mathbf{A}^{\prime}=\mathbf{A}_{2}^{\prime}\otimes\mathbf{A}_{1}^{\prime}
admitting the MARCF structural decomposition, where
\mathbf{A}_{i}=[\mathbf{C}_{i}~\mathbf{R}_{i}]\mathbf{D}_{i}[\mathbf{C}_{i}~\mathbf{P}_{i}]^{\top}
and
\mathbf{A}_{i}^{\prime}=[\mathbf{C}_{i}^{\prime}~\mathbf{R}_{i}^{\prime}]\mathbf{D}_{i}^{\prime}[\mathbf{C}_{i}^{\prime}~\mathbf{P}_{i}^{\prime}]^{\top}
, the following inequalities hold:

\begin{gathered}\mathrm{(RSC)}\quad\frac{\alpha}{2}\left\|\mathbf{A}-\mathbf{A}^{\prime}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\leq\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{A})-\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{A}^{\prime})-\left\langle\nabla\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{A}^{\prime}),\mathbf{A}-\mathbf{A}^{\prime}\right\rangle,\\
\mathrm{(RSS)}\quad\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{A})-\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{A}^{\prime})-\left\langle\nabla\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{A}^{\prime}),\mathbf{A}-\mathbf{A}^{\prime}\right\rangle\leq\frac{\beta}{2}\left\|\mathbf{A}-\mathbf{A}^{\prime}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2},\end{gathered}

(39)

The condition is a premise of Theorem
1
. As in
Nesterov, (2004)
, it implies that

\mathcal{L}\left(\mathbf{A}^{\prime}\right)-\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{A})\geq\left\langle\nabla\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{A}),\mathbf{A}^{\prime}-\mathbf{A}\right\rangle+\frac{1}{2\beta}\left\|\nabla\mathcal{L}\left(\mathbf{A}^{\prime}\right)-\nabla\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{A})\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}.

Combining this inequality with the RSC condition, we have

\displaystyle\left\langle\nabla\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{A})-\nabla\mathcal{L}\left(\mathbf{A}^{\prime}\right),\mathbf{A}-\mathbf{A}^{\prime}\right\rangle\geq\frac{\alpha}{2}\left\|\mathbf{A}-\mathbf{A}^{\prime}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\frac{1}{2\beta}\left\|\nabla\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{A})-\nabla\mathcal{L}\left(\mathbf{A}^{\prime}\right)\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2},

(40)

which is equivalent to the restricted correlated gradient (RCG) condition in
(
Han et al., 2022a,
)
.

The second and the third conditions ensure that the estimates remain within a small neighborhood of the true values during all iterations. They are established inductively in the proof.
Let
\underline{\sigma}:=\min\{\sigma_{r_{1}}(\mathbf{A}_{1}^{*}),\sigma_{r_{2}}(\mathbf{A}_{2}^{*})\}
be the smallest value among all the non-zero singular values of
\mathbf{A}_{1}^{*}
and
\mathbf{A}_{2}^{*}
. Define
\phi:=\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}=\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}
and
\kappa:=\phi/\underline{\sigma}
. With the notations, the second condition is specified as

\mathrm{dist}^{2}_{(j)}\leq\frac{C_{D}\alpha\phi^{2/3}}{\beta\kappa^{2}},

(41)

and then

\mathrm{dist}^{2}_{(j)}\leq\frac{C_{D}\phi^{2/3}}{\kappa^{2}}\leq C_{D}\phi^{2/3},\text{ for all }j=0,1,2,\dots

(42)

where
C_{D}
is positive constant small enough to ensure local convergence.

For the decomposition of
\mathbf{A}_{i}^{*}
, we require
[\mathbf{C}_{i}^{*}~\mathbf{R}_{i}^{*}]^{\top}[\mathbf{C}_{i}^{*}~\mathbf{R}_{i}^{*}]=b^{2}\mathbf{I}_{r_{i}}
,
[\mathbf{C}_{i}^{*}~\mathbf{P}_{i}^{*}]^{\top}[\mathbf{C}_{i}^{*}~\mathbf{P}_{i}^{*}]=b^{2}\mathbf{I}_{r_{i}},
for
i=1,2
. For simplicity, we consider
b=\phi^{1/3}
, though our proof can be readily extended to the case where
b\asymp\phi^{1/3}
.

The third condition is:

\begin{gathered}\left\|\left[\mathbf{C}_{i}^{(j)}~\mathbf{R}_{i}^{(j)}\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq(1+c_{b})b,\quad\left\|\left[\mathbf{C}_{i}^{(j)}~\mathbf{R}_{i}^{(j)}\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq(1+c_{b})b,\\
\left\|\mathbf{D}_{i}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq\frac{(1+c_{b})\phi}{b^{2}},\quad\forall i=1,2,\text{ and }j=0,1,2,...\end{gathered}

(43)

By sub-multiplicative property of Frobenius norm,
\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq(1+c_{a})\phi
and
\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq(1+c_{a})\phi
with
c_{b}
and
c_{a}
being two positive constants. We assume that
c_{b}\leq 0.01
and then
c_{a}\leq 0.04
. In fact, the constant
0.01
reflects the accuracy of the initial estimate, which can be replaced by any small positive numbers.

Finally, we give an outline of the proof of Theorem
1
, which proceeds by induction. Assuming that (
41
) and (
43
) hold for
\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{(j)}
, based on on RSC and RSS conditions, we derive an recursive relationship between
\mathrm{dist}^{2}_{(j+1)}
and
\mathrm{dist}^{2}_{(j)}
with an extra statistical error term. Then, we prove that
(
41
)
and
(
43
)
hold for
\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{(j+1)}
and verify the conditions for
\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{(0)}
, thereby finishing the inductive argument. Finally, built on the recursive relationship, we establish the three upper bounds presented Theorem
1
.

Specifically, in Steps 2-4, we focus on the update at
(j+1)
-th iterate to establish a recursive inequality between
\mathrm{dist}_{(j+1)}^{2}
and
\mathrm{dist}_{(j)}^{2}
, given (
40
), (
41
), and (
43
). In Step 2, for
\mathbf{R}_{1}
, we use the rule
\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j+1)}=\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}-\eta\nabla_{\mathbf{R}_{1}}\overline{\mathcal{L}}
and upper bound the estimation error of
\mathbf{R}^{(j+1)}
as

\begin{split}\min_{\mathbf{O}_{1,r}\in\mathbb{O}^{r_{1}-d_{1}}}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j+1)}-\mathbf{R}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,r}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\leq&\min_{\mathbf{O}_{1,r}\in\mathbb{O}^{r_{1}-d_{1}}}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{R}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,r}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\\
&-2\eta Q_{\mathbf{R}_{1},1}-2\lambda_{1}\eta G_{\mathbf{R}_{1}}-2\lambda_{2}\eta T_{\mathbf{R}_{1}}+\eta^{2}Q_{\mathbf{R}_{1},2}.\end{split}

(44)

Similarly, we do the same for
\mathbf{P}_{i}^{(j)},\mathbf{C}_{i}^{(j)}
and
\mathbf{D}_{i}^{(j)}
and sum them up to obtain the following first-stage upper bound, which is an informal version of (
68
).

\displaystyle\mathrm{dist}^{2}_{(j+1)}

\displaystyle\leq\mathrm{dist}^{2}_{(j)}+\eta^{2}Q_{2}-2\eta Q_{1}-2\lambda_{1}\eta G-2\lambda_{2}\eta T.

(45)

In Steps 3.1-3.4, we give further the lower bounds for
Q_{1}
,
G
, and
T
, whose coefficients are
-2\eta
, and the upper bound for
Q_{2}
, whose coefficient is
\eta^{2}
. They lead to an intermediate upper bound in (
83
), whose right hand side is negatively correlated to the estimation error of
\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\otimes\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}
. Thus, in Step 3.5, we construct a lower bound with respect to
\mathrm{dist}^{2}_{(j)}
and the regularization terms,
\mathcal{R}_{1}(\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{(j)})^{2}
and
\mathcal{R}_{2}({\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{(j)}})^{2}
. Plugging it into the intermediate bound, we have the second-stage upper bound of
\mathrm{dist}_{(j+1)}^{2}
as follows, which is an informal version of (
89
)

\displaystyle\mathrm{dist}^{2}_{(j+1)}\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-2\eta\rho_{1}\right)\mathrm{dist}^{2}_{(j)}+\rho_{2}\xi^{2}

(46)

\displaystyle+\rho_{3}\left\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\rho_{4}\mathcal{R}_{2}\left({\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{(j)}}\right)^{2}+\rho_{5}\mathcal{R}_{1}\left(\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{(j)}\right)^{2}.

In the above inequality,
\{\rho_{i}\}_{i=1}^{5}
are coefficients related to
\eta
,
\lambda_{1}
,
\lambda_{2}
,
\alpha
,
\beta
,
\phi
, and
\kappa
.

Next, in Step 4, we impose some sufficient conditions on
\eta
,
\lambda_{1},\lambda_{2}
,
\alpha
, and
\beta
for algorithm convergence. Then, we obtain the final recursive relationship (
90
):

\mathrm{dist}^{2}_{(j+1)}\leq(1-C_{0}\eta_{0}\alpha\beta^{-1}\kappa^{-2})\mathrm{dist}^{2}_{(j)}+C\kappa^{4}\alpha^{-2}\phi^{-10/3}\xi^{2}.

(47)

To complete the inductive argument, in Step 5, we first verify the conditions (
41
) and (
43
) for
\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{(0)}
. Given that (
41
) is an assumption of Theorem 3, it suffices to verify (
43
). Then, we prove that both conditions hold for
\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{(j+1)}
and complete the induction. Finally, in Step 6, we establish the three upper bounds presented in Theorem
1
based on the recursive relationship and the approximate equivalence of the three metrics, thereby completing the whole proof.

Step 2.
(
Upper bound of
\mathrm{dist}^{2}_{(j+1)}-\mathrm{dist}^{2}_{(j)}
)

By definition,

\begin{split}&\mathrm{dist}^{2}_{(j+1)}\\
=&\sum_{i=1,2}\Big\{\|\mathbf{R}_{i}^{(j+1)}-\mathbf{R}_{i}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,r}^{(j+1)}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}+\|\mathbf{P}_{i}^{(j+1)}-\mathbf{P}_{i}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,p}^{(j+1)}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}+\|\mathbf{C}_{i}^{(j+1)}-\mathbf{C}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,c}^{(j+1)}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\\
&+\|\mathbf{D}_{i}^{(j+1)}-\mathbf{O}_{1,u}^{(j+1)\top}\mathbf{D}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,v}^{(j+1)}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\Big\}\\
\leq&\sum_{i=1,2}\Big\{\|\mathbf{R}_{i}^{(j+1)}-\mathbf{R}_{i}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,r}^{(j)}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}+\|\mathbf{P}_{i}^{(j+1)}-\mathbf{P}_{i}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,p}^{(j)}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}+\|\mathbf{C}_{i}^{(j+1)}-\mathbf{C}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,c}^{(j)}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\\
&+\|\mathbf{D}_{i}^{(j+1)}-\mathbf{O}_{1,u}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{D}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,v}^{(j)}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\Big\}.\end{split}

(48)

In the following substeps, we derive upper bounds for the errors of
\mathbf{C},\mathbf{R},\mathbf{P}
and
\mathbf{D}
separately. Then, we combine them to give a first-stage upper bound (
68
).

Step 2.1
(Upper bounds for the errors of
\mathbf{R}_{i}
and
\mathbf{P}_{i}
)

By definition, we have

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j+1)}-\mathbf{R}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,r}^{(j)}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}

(49)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big\|\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{R}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,r}^{(j)}-\eta\Big(\nabla_{\mathbf{A}_{1}}\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)}][\mathbf{D}_{1,21}^{(j)}~\mathbf{D}_{1,22}^{(j)}]^{\top}

\displaystyle+\lambda_{1}\left(\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)\mathbf{A}_{i}^{(j)}[\mathbf{C}_{i}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{i}^{(j)}][\mathbf{D}_{1,21}^{(j)}~\mathbf{D}_{1,22}^{(j)}]^{\top}

\displaystyle+\lambda_{2}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}(\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}-b^{2}\mathbf{I}_{r_{1}-d_{1}})+\lambda_{2}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}\Big)\Big\|_{\text{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{R}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,r}^{(j)}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}+\eta^{2}\Big\|\nabla_{\mathbf{A}_{1}}\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)}][\mathbf{D}_{1,21}^{(j)}~\mathbf{D}_{1,22}^{(j)}]^{\top}

\displaystyle+\lambda_{1}\left(\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)\mathbf{A}_{i}^{(j)}[\mathbf{C}_{i}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{i}^{(j)}][\mathbf{D}_{1,21}^{(j)}~\mathbf{D}_{1,22}^{(j)}]^{\top}

\displaystyle+\lambda_{2}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}(\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}-b^{2}\mathbf{I}_{r_{1}-d_{1}})+\lambda_{2}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}\Big\|_{\text{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle 2\eta\Big\langle\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{R}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,r}^{(j)},\nabla_{\mathbf{A}_{1}}\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)}][\mathbf{D}_{1,21}^{(j)}~\mathbf{D}_{1,22}^{(j)}]^{\top}\Big\rangle

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle 2\lambda_{1}\eta\left\langle\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{R}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,r}^{(j)},\left(\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)\mathbf{A}_{i}^{(j)}[\mathbf{C}_{i}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{i}^{(j)}][\mathbf{D}_{1,21}^{(j)}~\mathbf{D}_{1,22}^{(j)}]^{\top}\right\rangle

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle 2\lambda_{2}\eta\Big\langle\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{R}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,r}^{(j)},\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}(\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}-b^{2}\mathbf{I}_{r_{1}-d_{1}})\Big\rangle

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle 2\lambda_{2}\eta\Big\langle\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{R}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,r}^{(j)},\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}\Big\rangle

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{R}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,r}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\eta^{2}I_{\mathbf{R}_{1},2}-2\eta I_{\mathbf{R}_{1},1}.

For
I_{\mathbf{R}_{1},2}
(the second term in (
49
)), by Cauchy’s inequality,

\displaystyle I_{\mathbf{R}_{1},2}=

\displaystyle\Big\|\nabla_{\mathbf{A}_{1}}\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)}][\mathbf{D}_{1,21}^{(j)}~\mathbf{D}_{1,22}^{(j)}]^{\top}

(50)

\displaystyle+\lambda_{1}\left(\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)\mathbf{A}_{i}^{(j)}[\mathbf{C}_{i}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{i}^{(j)}][\mathbf{D}_{1,21}^{(j)}~\mathbf{D}_{1,22}^{(j)}]^{\top}

\displaystyle+\lambda_{2}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}(\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}-b^{2}\mathbf{I}_{r_{1}-d_{1}})+\lambda_{2}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}\Big\|_{\text{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\|\nabla_{\mathbf{A}_{1}}\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)}][\mathbf{D}_{1,21}^{(j)}~\mathbf{D}_{1,22}^{(j)}]^{\top}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle+4\lambda_{1}^{2}\left(\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)^{2}\left\|\mathbf{A}_{i}^{(j)}[\mathbf{C}_{i}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{i}^{(j)}][\mathbf{D}_{1,21}^{(j)}~\mathbf{D}_{1,22}^{(j)}]^{\top}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle+4\lambda_{2}^{2}\|\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}(\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}-b^{2}\mathbf{I}_{r_{1}-d_{1}})\|_{\text{F}}^{2}+4\lambda_{2}^{2}\|\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}\|_{\text{F}}^{2},

where the first term in the RHS of
I_{\mathbf{R}_{1},2}
can be bounded by

\begin{split}&\|\nabla_{\mathbf{A}_{1}}\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)}][\mathbf{D}_{1,21}^{(j)}~\mathbf{D}_{1,22}^{(j)}]^{\top}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\\
=&\|\text{mat}(\mathcal{P}(\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)}))^{\top}\text{vec}(\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}))[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)}][\mathbf{D}_{1,21}^{(j)}~\mathbf{D}_{1,22}^{(j)}]^{\top}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\\
\leq&2\|\text{mat}(\mathcal{P}(\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*}))^{\top}\text{vec}(\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}))[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)}][\mathbf{D}_{1,21}^{(j)}~\mathbf{D}_{1,22}^{(j)}]^{\top}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\\
&+2\|\text{mat}(\mathcal{P}(\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*}))^{\top}\text{vec}(\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}))[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)}][\mathbf{D}_{1,21}^{(j)}~\mathbf{D}_{1,22}^{(j)}]^{\top}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}.\\
\end{split}

(51)

By duality of the Frobenius norm and definition of
\xi
, we have

\begin{split}&\Big\|\text{mat}(\mathcal{P}(\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*}))^{\top}\text{vec}(\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}))[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)}][\mathbf{D}_{1,21}^{(j)}~\mathbf{D}_{1,22}^{(j)}]^{\top}\Big\|_{\text{F}}\\
=&\sup_{\|\mathbf{W}\|_{\text{F}}=1}\left\langle\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*}),\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\otimes[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{W}]\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{0}&\mathbf{0}\\
\mathbf{D}_{1,21}^{(j)}&\mathbf{D}_{1,22}^{(j)}\end{bmatrix}[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)}]^{\top}\right\rangle\\
\leq&\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\|_{\text{F}}\cdot\|[\mathbf{D}_{1,21}^{(j)}~\mathbf{D}_{1,22}^{(j)}]\|_{\text{op}}\cdot\|[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)}]\|_{\text{op}}\cdot\xi(r_{1},r_{2},d_{1},d_{2}).\end{split}

(52)

Thus, the first term can be bounded by

\begin{split}&\|\nabla_{\mathbf{A}_{1}}\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)}][\mathbf{D}_{1,21}^{(j)}~\mathbf{D}_{1,22}^{(j)}]^{\top}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\\
\leq&2\|\text{mat}(\mathcal{P}(\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*}))^{\top}\text{vec}(\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}))[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)}][\mathbf{D}_{1,21}^{(j)}~\mathbf{D}_{1,22}^{(j)}]^{\top}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\\
&+2\|\text{mat}(\mathcal{P}(\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*}))^{\top}\text{vec}(\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}))\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\cdot\|[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)}]\|_{\text{op}}^{2}\cdot\|[\mathbf{D}_{1,21}^{(j)}~\mathbf{D}_{1,22}^{(j)}]\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\\
\leq&4b^{-2}\phi^{4}\cdot[\xi^{2}(r_{1},r_{2},d_{1},d_{2})+\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\|_{\text{F}}^{2}].\end{split}

(53)

The second term can be bounded by

\displaystyle\lambda_{1}^{2}\left(\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)^{2}\left\|\mathbf{A}_{i}^{(j)}[\mathbf{C}_{i}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{i}^{(j)}][\mathbf{D}_{1,21}^{(j)}~\mathbf{D}_{1,22}^{(j)}]^{\top}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

(54)

\displaystyle\leq 2\lambda_{1}^{2}b^{-2}\phi^{4}\left(\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)^{2}.

The third term can be bounded by

\begin{split}&\lambda_{2}^{2}\|\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}(\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}-b^{2}\mathbf{I}_{r_{1}-d_{1}})\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\leq\lambda_{2}^{2}\|\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}\|_{\text{op}}^{2}\|\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}-b^{2}\mathbf{I}_{r_{1}-d_{1}}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\\
\leq&2\lambda_{2}^{2}b^{2}\|\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}-b^{2}\mathbf{I}_{r_{1}-d_{1}}\|_{\text{F}}^{2},\end{split}

(55)

and the fourth term can be bounded by

\lambda_{2}^{2}\|\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\leq 2\lambda_{2}^{2}b^{2}\|\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}.

(56)

Combining these four upper bounds, we have

\begin{split}I_{\mathbf{R}_{1},2}\leq&16b^{-2}\phi^{4}\left(\xi^{2}(r_{1},r_{2},d_{1},d_{2})+\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\right)\\
&+8\lambda_{2}^{2}b^{2}\left(\|\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}-b^{2}\mathbf{I}_{r_{1}-d_{1}}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}+\|\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\right)\\
&+8\lambda_{1}^{2}b^{-2}\phi^{4}\left(\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)^{2}\\
:=&Q_{\mathbf{R}_{1},2}.\end{split}

(57)

For
I_{\mathbf{R}_{1},1}
defined in (
49
), rewrite its first term:

\begin{split}&\left\langle\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{R}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,r}^{(j)},\nabla_{\mathbf{A}_{1}}\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)}][\mathbf{D}_{1,21}^{(j)}~\mathbf{D}_{1,22}^{(j)}]^{\top}\right\rangle\\
=&\left\langle\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}[\mathbf{D}_{1,21}^{(j)}~\mathbf{D}_{1,22}^{(j)}][\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)}]^{\top}-\mathbf{R}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,r}^{(j)}[\mathbf{D}_{1,21}^{(j)}~\mathbf{D}_{1,22}^{(j)}][\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)}]^{\top},\nabla_{\mathbf{A}_{1}}\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})\right\rangle\\
:=&\left\langle\mathbf{A}_{1,r}^{(j)},\nabla_{\mathbf{A}_{1}}\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})\right\rangle\\
=&\left\langle\mathbf{A}_{1,r}^{(j)},\text{mat}(\mathcal{P}(\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)}))^{\top}\text{vec}(\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}))\right\rangle\\
=&\left\langle\mathrm{vec}(\mathbf{A}_{1,r}^{(j)}),\mathcal{P}(\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)}))^{\top}\mathrm{vec}(\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)})\right\rangle\\
=&\left\langle\mathrm{vec}(\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)})\mathrm{vec}(\mathbf{A}_{1,r}^{(j)})^{\top},\mathcal{P}(\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)}))\right\rangle\\
=&\left\langle\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\otimes\mathbf{A}_{1,r}^{(j)},\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})\right\rangle\\
:=&Q_{\mathbf{R}_{1},1}\end{split}

(58)

For the second term of
I_{\mathbf{R}_{1},1}
, define

G_{\mathbf{R}_{1}}:=\left\langle\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{R}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,r}^{(j)},\left(\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)\mathbf{A}_{i}^{(j)}[\mathbf{C}_{i}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{i}^{(j)}][\mathbf{D}_{1,21}^{(j)}~\mathbf{D}_{1,22}^{(j)}]^{\top}\right\rangle.

(59)

For the third and fourth terms of
I_{\mathbf{R}_{1},1}
, define

T_{\mathbf{R}_{1}}:=\left\langle\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{R}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,r}^{(j)},\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}(\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}-b^{2}\mathbf{I}_{r_{1}-d_{1}})+\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}\right\rangle.

(60)

Therefore, we can rewrite
I_{\mathbf{R}_{1},1}
as

I_{\mathbf{R}_{1},1}=Q_{\mathbf{R}_{1},1}+\lambda_{1}G_{\mathbf{R}_{1}}+\lambda_{2}T_{\mathbf{R}_{1}}.

(61)

Combining the bound for the
I_{\mathbf{R}_{1},2}
in (
57
), we have

\|\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j+1)}-\mathbf{R}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,r}^{(j)}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}-\|\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{R}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,r}^{(j)}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\leq-2\eta Q_{\mathbf{R}_{1},1}-2\lambda_{1}\eta G_{\mathbf{R}_{1}}-2\lambda_{2}\eta T_{\mathbf{R}_{1}}+\eta^{2}Q_{\mathbf{R}_{1},2}.

(62)

Similarly, for
\mathbf{R}_{2}^{(j+1)}
,
\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j+1)}
, and
\mathbf{P}_{2}^{(j+1)}
, we can define the similar quantities and show that

\begin{split}&\|\mathbf{R}_{2}^{(j+1)}-\mathbf{R}_{2}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{2,r}^{(j)}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}-\|\mathbf{R}_{2}^{(j)}-\mathbf{R}_{2}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{2,r}^{(j)}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\leq-2\eta Q_{\mathbf{R}_{2},1}-2\lambda_{1}\eta G_{\mathbf{R}_{2}}-2\lambda_{2}\eta T_{\mathbf{R}_{2}}+\eta^{2}Q_{\mathbf{R}_{2},2},\\
&\|\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j+1)}-\mathbf{P}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,p}^{(j)}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}-\|\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{P}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,p}^{(j)}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\leq-2\eta Q_{\mathbf{P}_{1},1}-2\lambda_{1}\eta G_{\mathbf{P}_{1}}-2\lambda_{2}\eta T_{\mathbf{P}_{1}}+\eta^{2}Q_{\mathbf{P}_{1},2},\\
\text{and }&\|\mathbf{P}_{2}^{(j+1)}-\mathbf{P}_{2}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{2,p}^{(j)}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}-\|\mathbf{P}_{2}^{(j)}-\mathbf{P}_{2}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{2,p}^{(j)}\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\leq-2\eta Q_{\mathbf{P}_{2},1}-2\lambda_{1}\eta G_{\mathbf{P}_{2}}-2\lambda_{2}\eta T_{\mathbf{P}_{2}}+\eta^{2}Q_{\mathbf{P}_{2},2}.\end{split}

(63)

Step 2.2
(Upper bound for the errors of
\mathbf{C}_{i}
)

For
\mathbf{C}_{1}
, note that

\nabla_{\mathbf{C}_{i}}\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}^{(j)}=\nabla_{\mathbf{A}_{i}}\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}^{(j)}(\mathbf{C}_{i}^{(j)}\mathbf{D}_{i,11}^{(j)\top}+\mathbf{P}_{i}^{(j)}\mathbf{D}_{i,12}^{(j)\top})+\nabla_{\mathbf{A}_{i}}\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}^{(j)\top}(\mathbf{C}_{i}^{(j)}\mathbf{D}_{i,11}^{(j)}+\mathbf{R}_{i}^{(j)}\mathbf{D}_{i,21}^{(j)}),

(64)

and then,

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j+1)}-\mathbf{C}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,c}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

(65)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{C}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,c}^{(j)}-\eta\Big\{\nabla_{\mathbf{C}_{1}}\mathcal{L}^{(j)}+\lambda_{1}\left(\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)\mathbf{A}_{i}^{(j)}[\mathbf{C}_{i}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{i}^{(j)}][\mathbf{D}_{i,11}^{(j)}~\mathbf{D}_{i,12}^{(j)}]^{\top}\right.

\displaystyle+\lambda_{1}\left(\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)\mathbf{A}_{i}^{(j)^{\top}}[\mathbf{C}_{i}^{(j)}~\mathbf{R}_{i}^{(j)}][\mathbf{D}_{i,11}^{(j)^{\top}}~\mathbf{D}_{i,21}^{(j)\top}]^{\top}

\displaystyle\left.+2\lambda_{2}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}\left(\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}-b^{2}\mathbf{I}_{d_{1}}\right)+\lambda_{2}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}+\lambda_{2}\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)}\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}\Big\}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{C}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,c}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\eta^{2}\bigg\|\nabla_{\mathbf{C}_{1}}\mathcal{L}^{(j)}+\lambda_{1}\left(\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)\mathbf{A}_{i}^{(j)}[\mathbf{C}_{i}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{i}^{(j)}][\mathbf{D}_{i,11}^{(j)}~\mathbf{D}_{i,12}^{(j)}]^{\top}

\displaystyle+\lambda_{1}\left(\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)\mathbf{A}_{i}^{(j)^{\top}}[\mathbf{C}_{i}^{(j)}~\mathbf{R}_{i}^{(j)}][\mathbf{D}_{i,11}^{(j)^{\top}}~\mathbf{D}_{i,21}^{(j)\top}]^{\top}

\displaystyle+2\lambda_{2}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}\left(\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}-b^{2}\mathbf{I}_{d_{1}}\right)+\lambda_{2}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}+\lambda_{2}\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)}\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}\bigg\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle 2\eta\left\langle\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{C}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,c}^{(j)},\nabla_{\mathbf{C}_{1}}\mathcal{L}^{(j)}\right\rangle

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle 2\lambda_{1}\eta\left\langle\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{C}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,c}^{(j)},\left(\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)\mathbf{A}_{i}^{(j)}[\mathbf{C}_{i}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{i}^{(j)}][\mathbf{D}_{i,11}^{(j)}~\mathbf{D}_{i,12}^{(j)}]^{\top}\right\rangle

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle 2\lambda_{1}\eta\left\langle\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{C}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,c}^{(j)},\left(\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)\mathbf{A}_{i}^{(j)^{\top}}[\mathbf{C}_{i}^{(j)}~\mathbf{R}_{i}^{(j)}][\mathbf{D}_{i,11}^{(j)^{\top}}~\mathbf{D}_{i,21}^{(j)\top}]^{\top}\right\rangle

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle 2\lambda_{2}\eta\left\langle\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{C}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,c}^{(j)},2\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}\left(\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}-b^{2}\mathbf{I}_{d_{1}}\right)\right\rangle

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle 2\lambda_{2}\eta\left\langle\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{C}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,c}^{(j)},\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}+\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)}\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}\right\rangle

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{C}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,c}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\eta^{2}I_{\mathbf{C}_{1},2}-2\eta I_{\mathbf{C}_{1},1}.

For
I_{\mathbf{C}_{1},2}
in (
65
),

\displaystyle I_{\mathbf{C}_{1},2}\leq

\displaystyle 5\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{C}_{1}}\mathcal{L}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+40\lambda_{1}^{2}b^{-2}\phi^{4}\left(\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+40\lambda_{2}^{2}b^{2}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}-b^{2}\mathbf{I}_{d_{1}}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+10\lambda_{2}^{2}b^{2}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+10\lambda_{2}^{2}b^{2}\left\|\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}.

The first term of the RHS can be bounded as

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{C}_{1}}\mathcal{L}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{A}_{1}}\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)}\right]\left[\mathbf{D}_{1,11}^{(j)}~\mathbf{D}_{1,12}^{(j)}\right]^{\top}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle+2\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{A}_{1}}\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})^{\top}\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}\right]\left[\mathbf{D}_{1,11}^{(j)\top}~\mathbf{D}_{1,21}^{(j)\top}\right]^{\top}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=2\left\|\text{mat}\left(\mathcal{P}\left(\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}\left(\mathbf{A}^{(j)}\right)\right)^{\top}\mathrm{vec}(\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right))\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)}\right]\left[\mathbf{D}_{1,11}^{(j)}~\mathbf{D}_{1,12}^{(j)}\right]^{\top}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle+2\left\|\text{mat}\left(\mathcal{P}\left(\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}\left(\mathbf{A}^{(j)}\right)\right)^{\top}\mathrm{vec}(\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)})\right)^{\top}\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}\right]\left[\mathbf{D}_{1,11}^{(j)\top}~\mathbf{D}_{1,21}^{(j)\top}\right]^{\top}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 4\left\|\text{mat}\left(\mathcal{P}\left(\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}\left(\mathbf{A}^{*}\right)\right)^{\top}\mathrm{vec}(\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)})\right)\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)}\right]\left[\mathbf{D}_{1,11}^{(j)}~\mathbf{D}_{1,12}^{(j)}\right]^{\top}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle+4\left\|\text{mat}\left(\mathcal{P}\left(\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}\left(\mathbf{A}^{*}\right)\right)^{\top}\mathrm{vec}(\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right))^{\top}\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}\right]\left[\mathbf{D}_{1,11}^{(j)\top}~\mathbf{D}_{1,21}^{(j)\top}\right]^{\top}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle+4\left\|\text{mat}\left(\mathcal{P}\left(\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\right)^{\top}\mathrm{vec}(\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)})\right)\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)}\right]\left[\mathbf{D}_{1,11}^{(j)}~\mathbf{D}_{1,12}^{(j)}\right]^{\top}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle+4\left\|\text{mat}\left(\mathcal{P}\left(\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\right)^{\top}\mathrm{vec}(\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)})\right)^{\top}\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}\right]\left[\mathbf{D}_{1,11}^{(j)\top}~\mathbf{D}_{1,21}^{(j)\top}\right]^{\top}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 16b^{-2}\phi^{4}\xi^{2}(r_{1},r_{2},d_{1},d_{2})+16b^{-2}\phi^{4}\left\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\right\|_{\text{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=16b^{-2}\phi^{4}\left(\xi^{2}(r_{1},r_{2},d_{1},d_{2})+\left\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\right\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\right).

Thus,
I_{\mathbf{C}_{1},2}
can be upper bounded by

\displaystyle I_{\mathbf{C}_{1},2}\leq

\displaystyle 80b^{-2}\phi^{4}\left(\xi^{2}(r_{1},r_{2},d_{1},d_{2})+\left\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\right\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\right)

(66)

\displaystyle+40\lambda_{1}^{2}b^{-2}\phi^{4}\left(\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+40\lambda_{1}^{2}b^{2}\left\|\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}-b^{2}\mathbf{I}_{d_{1}}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+10a^{2}b^{2}\left\|\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+10\lambda_{1}^{2}b^{2}\left\|\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle Q_{\mathbf{C}_{1},2}.

For
I_{\mathbf{C}_{1},1}
in (
65
), similarly to
\mathbf{R}_{1}
step, we rewrite its first term as the following:

\displaystyle\left\langle\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{C}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,c}^{(j)},\nabla_{\mathbf{C}_{1}}\mathcal{L}^{(j)}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\langle\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}\mathbf{D}_{1,21}^{(j)}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)\top}-\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}\mathbf{D}_{1,21}^{(j)}\mathbf{O}_{1,c}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{*\top},\nabla_{\mathbf{A}_{1}}\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}\left(\mathbf{A}^{(j)}\right)\right\rangle

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\left\langle\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}\mathbf{D}_{1,12}^{(j)}\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)\top}-\mathbf{C}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,c}^{(j)}\mathbf{D}_{1,12}^{(j)}\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)\top},\nabla_{\mathbf{A}_{1}}\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}\left(\mathbf{A}^{(j)}\right)\right\rangle

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\left\langle\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}\mathbf{D}_{1,11}^{(j)}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)\top}-\mathbf{C}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,c}^{(j)}\mathbf{D}_{1,11}^{(j)}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)\top},\nabla_{\mathbf{A}_{1}}\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}\left(\mathbf{A}^{(j)}\right)\right\rangle

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\left\langle\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}\mathbf{D}_{1,11}^{(j)}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)\top}-\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}\mathbf{D}_{1,11}^{(j)}\mathbf{O}_{1,c}^{(j)}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{*\top},\nabla_{\mathbf{A}_{1}}\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}\left(\mathbf{A}^{(j)}\right)\right\rangle

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\left\langle\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{C}_{1}}^{(j)},\nabla_{\mathbf{A}_{1}}\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}\left(\mathbf{A}^{(j)}\right)\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\langle\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\otimes\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{C}_{1}}^{(j)},\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})\right\rangle

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle Q_{\mathbf{C}_{1},1}.

For the last three terms, denote

\displaystyle G_{\mathbf{C}_{1}}:=

\displaystyle\left\langle\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{C}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,c}^{(j)},\left(\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)\mathbf{A}_{i}^{(j)}[\mathbf{C}_{i}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{i}^{(j)}][\mathbf{D}_{i,11}^{(j)}~\mathbf{D}_{i,12}^{(j)}]^{\top}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\left\langle\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{C}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,c}^{(j)},\left(\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)\mathbf{A}_{i}^{(j)\top}[\mathbf{C}_{i}^{(j)}~\mathbf{R}_{i}^{(j)}][\mathbf{D}_{i,11}^{(j)\top}~\mathbf{D}_{i,21}^{(j)\top}]^{\top}\right\rangle

and

T_{\mathbf{C}_{1}}:=\left\langle\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{C}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,c}^{(j)},2\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}\left(\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}-b^{2}\mathbf{I}_{d_{1}}\right)+\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}+\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)}\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}\right\rangle.

Therefore, we can rewrite
I_{\mathbf{C}_{1},1}
as

I_{\mathbf{C}_{1},1}=Q_{\mathbf{C}_{1},1}+\lambda_{1}G_{\mathbf{C}_{1}}+\lambda_{2}T_{\mathbf{C}_{1}}.

Combining these bounds for
I_{\mathbf{C}_{1},2}
in (
66
), we have

\left\|\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j+1)}-\mathbf{C}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,c}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{C}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,c}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\leq-2\eta Q_{\mathbf{C}_{1},1}-2\lambda_{1}\eta G_{\mathbf{C}_{1},1}-2\lambda_{2}\eta T_{\mathbf{C}_{1},1}+\eta^{2}Q_{\mathbf{C}_{1},2}.

Similarly, we also have

\left\|\mathbf{C}_{2}^{(j+1)}-\mathbf{C}_{2}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{2,c}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{C}_{2}^{(j)}-\mathbf{C}_{2}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{2,c}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\leq-2\eta Q_{\mathbf{C}_{2},1}-2\lambda_{1}\eta G_{\mathbf{C}_{2},1}-2\lambda_{2}\eta T_{\mathbf{C}_{2},1}+\eta^{2}Q_{\mathbf{C}_{2},2}.

Step 2.3
(Upper bound for the errors of
\mathbf{D}_{i}
)

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{D}_{1}^{(j+1)}-\mathbf{O}_{1,u}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{D}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,v}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

(67)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{D}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{O}_{1,u}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{D}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,v}^{(j)}-\eta\left\{\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}\right]^{\top}\nabla_{\mathbf{A}_{1}}\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)}\right]\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.+\lambda_{1}\left(\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)[\mathbf{C}_{i}^{(j)}~\mathbf{R}_{i}^{(j)}]^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{i}^{(j)}[\mathbf{C}_{i}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{i}^{(j)}]\right\}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{D}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{O}_{1,u}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{D}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,v}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\eta^{2}\left\|\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}\right]^{\top}\nabla_{\mathbf{A}_{1}}\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)}\right]\right.

\displaystyle+\left.\lambda_{1}\left(\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)[\mathbf{C}_{i}^{(j)}~\mathbf{R}_{i}^{(j)}]^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{i}^{(j)}[\mathbf{C}_{i}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{i}^{(j)}]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle 2\eta\left\langle\mathbf{D}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{O}_{1,u}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{D}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,v}^{(j)},\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}\right]^{\top}\nabla_{\mathbf{A}_{1}}\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)}\right]\right\rangle

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle 2\lambda_{1}\eta\left\langle\mathbf{D}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{O}_{1,u}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{D}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,v}^{(j)},\left(\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)\left[\mathbf{C}_{i}^{(j)}~\mathbf{R}_{i}^{(j)}\right]^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{i}^{(j)}\left[\mathbf{C}_{i}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{i}^{(j)}\right]\right\rangle

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{D}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{O}_{1,u}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{D}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,v}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\eta^{2}I_{\mathbf{D}_{1},2}-2\eta I_{\mathbf{D}_{1},1}.

For
I_{\mathbf{D}_{1},2}
:

\displaystyle I_{\mathbf{D}_{1},2}\leq

\displaystyle 2\left\|\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}\right]^{\top}\nabla_{\mathbf{A}_{1}}\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)}\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+4\lambda_{1}^{2}b^{4}\phi^{2}\left(\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)^{2}.

For the first term,

\displaystyle\left\|\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}\right]^{\top}\nabla_{\mathbf{A}_{1}}\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)}\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}\right]^{\top}\text{mat}\left(\mathcal{P}\left(\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}\left(\mathbf{A}^{(j)}\right)\right)^{\top}\mathrm{vec}(\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right))\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)}\right]\right\|_{\text{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\left\|\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}\right]^{\top}\text{mat}\left(\mathcal{P}\left(\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}\left(\mathbf{A}^{*}\right)\right)^{\top}\mathrm{vec}(\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right))\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)}\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle 2\left\|\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}\right]^{\top}\text{mat}\left(\mathcal{P}\left[\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}\left(\mathbf{A}^{(j)}\right)-\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\right]^{\top}\mathrm{vec}(\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)})\right)\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)}\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4b^{4}\phi^{2}\left(\xi^{2}(r_{1},r_{2},d_{1},d_{2})+\left\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\right\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\right)

Then
I_{\mathbf{D}_{1},2}
can be upper bounded as

\displaystyle I_{\mathbf{D}_{1},2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4b^{4}\phi^{2}\left(\xi^{2}(r_{1},r_{2},d_{1},d_{2})+\left\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\right\|_{\text{F}}^{2}\right)+4\lambda_{1}^{2}b^{4}\phi^{2}\left(\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle Q_{\mathbf{D}_{1},2}

For
I_{\mathbf{D}_{1},1}
, similarly we define

\displaystyle\left\langle\mathbf{D}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{O}_{1,u}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{D}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,v}^{(j)},\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}\right]^{\top}\nabla_{\mathbf{A}_{1}}\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)}\right]\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\langle\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}-\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}\right]\mathbf{O}_{1,u}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{D}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,v}^{(j)}\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)}\right]^{\top},\nabla_{\mathbf{A}_{1}}\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})\right\rangle

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\left\langle\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{D}_{1}}^{(j)},\nabla_{\mathbf{A}_{1}}\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\langle\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\otimes\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{D}_{1}}^{(j)},\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})\right\rangle

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle Q_{\mathbf{D}_{1},1},

and

G_{\mathbf{D}_{1}}:=\left\langle\mathbf{D}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{O}_{1,u}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{D}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,v}^{(j)},\left(\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}\right]^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)}\right]\right\rangle

Hence, we have

\left\|\mathbf{D}_{1}^{(j+1)}-\mathbf{O}_{1,u}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{D}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,v}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{D}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{O}_{1,u}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{D}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,v}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\leq-2\eta Q_{\mathbf{D}_{1},1}-2\lambda_{1}\eta G_{\mathbf{D}_{1}}+\eta^{2}Q_{\mathbf{D}_{1},2},

and

\left\|\mathbf{D}_{2}^{(j+1)}-\mathbf{O}_{2,u}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{D}_{2}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{2,v}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{D}_{2}^{(j)}-\mathbf{O}_{2,u}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{D}_{2}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{2,v}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\leq-2\eta Q_{\mathbf{D}_{2},1}-2\lambda_{1}\eta G_{\mathbf{D}_{2}}+\eta^{2}Q_{\mathbf{D}_{2},2}.

Combining the pieces above,

\displaystyle\mathrm{dist}^{2}_{(j+1)}

\displaystyle\leq\mathrm{dist}^{2}_{(j)}+\eta^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\left(Q_{\mathrm{D}_{i},2}+Q_{\mathrm{R}_{i},2}+Q_{\mathrm{P}_{i},2}+Q_{\mathrm{C}_{i},2}\right)

(68)

\displaystyle-2\eta\sum_{i=1}^{2}\left(Q_{\mathrm{D}_{i},1}+Q_{\mathrm{R}_{i},1}+Q_{\mathrm{P}_{i},1}+Q_{\mathrm{C}_{i},1}\right)

\displaystyle-2\lambda_{1}\eta\sum_{i=1}^{2}\left(G_{D_{i}}+G_{R_{i}}+G_{P_{i}}+G_{C_{i}}\right)

\displaystyle-2\lambda_{2}\eta\sum_{i=1}^{2}\left(T_{R_{i}}+T_{P_{i}}+T_{C_{i}}\right).

Step 3.
(
Recursive relationship between
\mathrm{dist}^{2}_{(j+1)}
and
\mathrm{dist}^{2}_{(j)}
)

In this step, we will derive upper bounds of
Q_{\cdot,1},Q_{\cdot,2},G
and
T
terms, and finally obtain a upper bound as in (
89
).

Step 3.1
(Lower bound for
\sum_{i=1}^{2}\left(Q_{\mathbf{D}_{i},1}+Q_{\mathbf{R}_{i},1}+Q_{\mathbf{P}_{i},1}+Q_{\mathbf{C}_{i},1}\right)
)

By definition,

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2}\left(Q_{\mathbf{D}_{i},1}+Q_{\mathbf{R}_{i},1}+Q_{\mathbf{P}_{i},1}+Q_{\mathbf{C}_{i},1}\right)

(69)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\langle\begin{matrix}\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\otimes\left(\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{D}_{1}}^{(j)}+\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{R}_{1}}^{(j)}+\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{P}_{1}}^{(j)}+\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{C}_{1}}^{(j)}\right)\\
+\left(\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{D}_{2}}^{(j)}+\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{R}_{2}}^{(j)}+\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{P}_{2}}^{(j)}+\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{C}_{2}}^{(j)}\right)\otimes\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\end{matrix},\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}\left(\mathbf{A}^{(j)}\right)\right\rangle.

Noting that

\displaystyle\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{D}_{1}}^{(j)}+\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{R}_{1}}^{(j)}+\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{P}_{1}}^{(j)}+\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{C}_{1}}^{(j)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 3\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}-\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}\right]\mathbf{O}_{1,u}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{D}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,v}^{(j)}\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)}\right]^{\top}-\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}\right]\mathbf{D}_{1}^{(j)}\mathbf{O}_{1,v}^{(j)\top}\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{*}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{*}\right]^{\top}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle{\displaystyle\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{*}~\mathbf{R}_{1}^{*}\right]\mathbf{O}_{1,u}^{(j)}\mathbf{D}_{1}^{(j)}\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)}\right]^{\top}},

we define

\mathbf{H}_{1}^{(j)}:=\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\otimes\left(\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{D}_{1}}^{(j)}+\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{R}_{1}}^{(j)}+\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{P}_{1}}^{(j)}+\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{C}_{1}}^{(j)}\right)+\left(\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{D}_{2}}^{(j)}+\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{R}_{2}}^{(j)}+\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{P}_{2}}^{(j)}+\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{C}_{2}}^{(j)}\right)\otimes\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)},

which contains all the first order perturbation terms. By Lemma
C.1
, we know that

\mathbf{H}_{1}^{(j)}=\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\otimes\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{A}_{2}^{*}\otimes\mathbf{A}_{1}^{*}+\mathbf{H}^{(j)}.

Hence, (
69
) can be simplified as

\left\langle\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\otimes\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{A}_{2}^{*}\otimes\mathbf{A}_{1}^{*}+\mathbf{H}^{(j)},\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}\left(\mathbf{A}^{(j)}\right)\right\rangle.

With conditions (
43
) (
41
) and Lemma
C.1
, we have

\left\|\mathbf{H}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq C_{h}\phi^{4/3}\mathrm{dist}^{2}_{(j)},

where
C_{h}
is a constant of moderate size.

Then, for (
69
), with RCG condition (
40
), we have

\displaystyle\left\langle\begin{matrix}\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\otimes\left(\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{D}_{1}}^{(j)}+\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{R}_{1}}^{(j)}+\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{P}_{1}}^{(j)}+\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{C}_{1}}^{(j)}\right)\\
+\left(\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{D}_{2}}^{(j)}+\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{R}_{2}}^{(j)}+\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{P}_{2}}^{(j)}+\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{C}_{2}}^{(j)}\right)\otimes\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\end{matrix},\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}\left(\mathbf{A}^{(j)}\right)\right\rangle

(70)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\langle\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\otimes\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{A}_{2}^{*}\otimes\mathbf{A}_{1}^{*}+\mathbf{H}^{(j)},\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}\left(\mathbf{A}^{(j)}\right)\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\langle\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\otimes\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{A}_{2}^{*}\otimes\mathbf{A}_{1}^{*},\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\right\rangle+\left\langle\mathbf{H}^{(j)},\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\left\langle\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\otimes\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{A}_{2}^{*}\otimes\mathbf{A}_{1}^{*}+\mathbf{H}^{(j)},\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}\left(\mathbf{A}^{*}\right)\right\rangle

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{\alpha}{2}\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\otimes\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{A}_{2}^{*}\otimes\mathbf{A}_{1}^{*}\right\|_{F}^{2}+\frac{1}{2\beta}\left\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\right\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle-\left\|\mathbf{H}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\left\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle-\left|\left\langle\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\otimes\left(\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{D}_{1}}^{(j)}+\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{R}_{1}}^{(j)}+\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{P}_{1}}^{(j)}+\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{C}_{1}}^{(j)}\right),\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}\left(\mathbf{A}^{*}\right)\right\rangle\right|

\displaystyle-\left|\left\langle\left(\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{D}_{2}}^{(j)}+\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{R}_{2}}^{(j)}+\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{P}_{2}}^{(j)}+\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{C}_{2}}^{(j)}\right)\otimes\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)},\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}\left(\mathbf{A}^{*}\right)\right\rangle\right|.

For the fourth term in (
70
), plugging in
b=\phi^{1/3}
:

\displaystyle\left|\left\langle\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\otimes\left(\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{D}_{1}}^{(j)}+\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{R}_{1}}^{(j)}+\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{P}_{1}}^{(j)}+\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{C}_{1}}^{(j)}\right),\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}\left(\mathbf{A}^{*}\right)\right\rangle\right|

(71)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left|\left\langle\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\otimes\left(\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}-\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{*}~\mathbf{R}_{1}^{*}\right]\mathbf{O}_{1,u}^{(j)}\mathbf{D}_{1}^{(j)}\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)}\right]^{\top}\right),\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}\left(\mathbf{A}^{*}\right)\right\rangle\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\left\langle\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\otimes\left(\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}-\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}\right]\mathbf{D}_{1}^{(j)}\mathbf{O}_{1,v}^{(j)\top}\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{*}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{*}\right]^{\top}\right),\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}\left(\mathbf{A}^{*}\right)\right\rangle\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\left\langle\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\otimes\left(\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}-\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}\right]\mathbf{O}_{1,u}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{D}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,v}^{(j)}\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)}\right]^{\top}\right),\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}\left(\mathbf{A}^{*}\right)\right\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\xi(r_{1},r_{2},d_{1},d_{2})\left(\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\left\|\mathbf{D}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\text{op }}\cdot\left\|\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)}\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{op}}\cdot\left\|\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}\right]-\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{*}~\mathbf{R}_{1}^{*}\right]\mathbf{O}_{1,u}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right)

\displaystyle+\xi(r_{1},r_{2},d_{1},d_{2})\left(\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\left\|\mathbf{D}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\text{op }}\cdot\left\|\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{op}}\cdot\left\|\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)}\right]-\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{*}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{*}\right]\mathbf{O}_{1,v}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right)

\displaystyle+\xi(r_{1},r_{2},d_{1},d_{2})\left(\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\left\|\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{op}}\cdot\left\|\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)}\right]\right\|_{\mathrm{op}}\cdot\left\|\mathbf{D}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{O}_{1,u}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{D}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,v}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(2b^{2}+4\phi/b\right)\phi\xi(r_{1},r_{2},d_{1},d_{2})\mathrm{dist}_{(j)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 6\phi^{5/3}\xi(r_{1},r_{2},d_{1},d_{2})\mathrm{dist}_{(j)}.

Applying the same analysis on the last term in (
70
), the last two terms in (
70
) can be upper bounded by

\displaystyle\left|\left\langle\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\otimes\left(\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{D}_{1}}^{(j)}+\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{R}_{1}}^{(j)}+\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{P}_{1}}^{(j)}+\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{C}_{1}}^{(j)}\right),\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}\left(\mathbf{A}^{*}\right)\right\rangle\right|

(72)

\displaystyle+\left|\left\langle\left(\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{D}_{2}}^{(j)}+\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{R}_{2}}^{(j)}+\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{P}_{2}}^{(j)}+\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{C}_{2}}^{(j)}\right)\otimes\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)},\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}\left(\mathbf{A}^{*}\right)\right\rangle\right|

(73)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 6\phi^{5/3}\xi(r_{1},r_{2},d_{1},d_{2})\mathrm{dist}_{(j)}+6\phi^{5/3}\xi(r_{1},r_{2},d_{1},d_{2})\mathrm{dist}_{(j)}

(74)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 12\phi^{5/3}\xi(r_{1},r_{2},d_{1},d_{2})\mathrm{dist}_{(j)}

(75)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 36c\phi^{10/3}\mathrm{dist}^{2}_{(j)}+\frac{1}{c}\xi(r_{1},r_{2},d_{1},d_{2})^{2},\quad\forall c>0.

(76)

The last inequality stems from the fact that
x^{2}+y^{2}\geq 2xy
.

For the third term in (
70
), we know from Lemma
C.1
that
\left\|\mathbf{H}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq C_{h}\phi^{4/3}\mathrm{dist}^{2}_{(j)}
, and with condition (
41
),
\mathrm{dist}^{2}_{(j)}\leq C_{D}\phi^{2/3}\alpha\beta^{-1}\kappa^{-2}
, and

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{H}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\left\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4\beta}\left\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\beta\left\|\mathbf{H}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4\beta}\left\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\beta(C_{h}^{2}\phi^{8/3}\mathrm{dist}^{2}_{(j)})\mathrm{dist}^{2}_{(j)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4\beta}\left\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\frac{C_{H}\alpha\phi^{10/3}}{\kappa^{2}}\mathrm{dist}^{2}_{(j)},

where
C_{H}=C_{h}^{2}C_{D}
is a small positive constant.
Consequently, putting together the bounds, we have:

\displaystyle\left\langle\begin{matrix}\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\otimes\left(\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{D}_{1}}^{(j)}+\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{R}_{1}}^{(j)}+\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{P}_{1}}^{(j)}+\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{C}_{1}}^{(j)}\right)\\
+\left(\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{D}_{2}}^{(j)}+\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{R}_{2}}^{(j)}+\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{P}_{2}}^{(j)}+\mathbf{A}_{\mathbf{C}_{2}}^{(j)}\right)\otimes\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\end{matrix},\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}\left(\mathbf{A}^{(j)}\right)\right\rangle

(77)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{\alpha}{2}\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\otimes\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{A}_{2}^{*}\otimes\mathbf{A}_{1}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\frac{1}{4\beta}\left\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{C_{H}\alpha\phi^{10/3}}{\kappa^{2}}\mathrm{dist}^{2}_{(j)}-36c\phi^{10/3}\mathrm{dist}^{2}_{(j)}-\frac{1}{c}\xi(r_{1},r_{2},d_{1},d_{2})^{2}.

Step 3.2.
(Lower bound for
\sum_{i=1}^{2}\left(G_{\mathbf{D}_{i}}+G_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}+G_{\mathbf{P}_{i}}+G_{\mathbf{C}_{i}}\right)
)

Recall the definitions in (
59
) and (
63
). It can be easily verified that, by adding the terms together, we have

\displaystyle G_{\mathbf{D}_{1}}+G_{\mathbf{R}_{1}}+G_{\mathbf{P}_{1}}+G_{\mathbf{C}_{1}}

(78)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\langle\begin{matrix}\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}-\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{*}~\mathbf{R}_{1}^{*}\right]\mathbf{O}_{1,u}\mathbf{D}_{1}^{(j)}\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)}\right]^{\top}\\
+\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}-\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}\right]\mathbf{O}_{1,u}^{\top}\mathbf{D}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,v}\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)}\right]^{\top}\\
+\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}-\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}\right]\mathbf{D}_{1}^{(j)}\mathbf{O}_{1,v}^{\top}\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{*}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{*}\right]^{\top}\end{matrix},\left(\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\right\rangle

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\left\langle\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{A}_{1},1},\left(\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\right\rangle.

The left side of the inner product,
\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{A}_{1},1}
, contains the first order perturbation terms with respect to
\mathbf{A}_{1}
. By Lemma A.1 in
Wang et al., (2023)
, it is exactly
\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{A}_{1}^{*}+\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{A}_{1},2}
, where
\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{A}_{1},2}
comprises all the second and third-order perturbation terms. Applying it with
b=\phi^{1/3}
, we have
\left\|\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{A}_{1},2}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq 5\phi^{1/3}\mathrm{dist}^{2}_{(j)}
. Similarly for
\mathbf{A}_{2}
, we have

\displaystyle G_{\mathbf{D}_{2}}+G_{\mathbf{R}_{2}}+G_{\mathbf{P}_{2}}+G_{\mathbf{C}_{2}}

(79)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\langle\begin{matrix}\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}-\left[\mathbf{C}_{2}^{*}~\mathbf{R}_{2}^{*}\right]\mathbf{O}_{2,u}\mathbf{D}_{2}^{(j)}\left[\mathbf{C}_{2}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{2}^{(j)}\right]^{\top}\\
+\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}-\left[\mathbf{C}_{2}^{(j)}~\mathbf{R}_{2}^{(j)}\right]\mathbf{O}_{2,u}^{\top}\mathbf{D}_{2}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{2,v}\left[\mathbf{C}_{2}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{2}^{(j)}\right]^{\top}\\
+\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}-\left[\mathbf{C}_{2}^{(j)}~\mathbf{R}_{2}^{(j)}\right]\mathbf{D}_{2}^{(j)}\mathbf{O}_{2,v}^{\top}\left[\mathbf{C}_{2}^{*}~\mathbf{P}_{2}^{*}\right]^{\top}\end{matrix},-\left(\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\rangle

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\left\langle\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{A}_{2},1},-\left(\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\langle\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}-\mathbf{A}_{2}^{*}+\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{A}_{2},2},-\left(\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\rangle,

and
\left\|\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{A}_{2},2}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\leq 5\phi^{1/3}\mathrm{dist}^{2}_{(j)}
.

Now we define

\mathbf{Z}=\begin{pmatrix}\mathrm{vec}(\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)})\\
\mathrm{vec}(\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)})\end{pmatrix},\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}=\begin{pmatrix}\mathrm{vec}(\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)})\\
-\mathrm{vec}(\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)})\end{pmatrix},\mathbf{Z}^{*}=\begin{pmatrix}\mathrm{vec}(\mathbf{A}_{1}^{*})\\
\mathrm{vec}(\mathbf{A}_{2}^{*})\end{pmatrix},\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{*}=\begin{pmatrix}\mathrm{vec}(\mathbf{A}_{1}^{*})\\
-\mathrm{vec}(\mathbf{A}_{2}^{*})\end{pmatrix}.

Then, one can show that

\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}=\mathbf{Z}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}=\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}.

Vectorizing the matrices and putting (
78
) and (
79
) together, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2}\left(G_{\mathbf{D}_{i}}+G_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}+G_{\mathbf{P}_{i}}+G_{\mathbf{C}_{i}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\langle\mathbf{Z}-\mathbf{Z}^{*},\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\left\langle\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{A}_{1},2},\left(\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\right\rangle+\left\langle\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{A}_{2},2},-\left(\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\rangle.

The second and third terms can be lower bouned by

\displaystyle\left\langle\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{A}_{1},2},\left(\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\right\rangle+\left\langle\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{A}_{2},2},-\left(\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle-\left|\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right|\left(\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{A}_{1},2}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}+\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\left\|\mathbf{H}_{\mathbf{A}_{2},2}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\right)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle-20\phi^{4/3}\mathrm{dist}^{2}_{(j)}\left|\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right|.

For the first term, noting that
\mathbf{Z}^{*\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{*}=\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{*\top}\mathbf{Z}^{*}=0
and
\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}^{*}=\mathbf{Z}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{*}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\langle\mathbf{Z}-\mathbf{Z}^{*},\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}-\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}^{*},\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\left\|\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\left\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}-2\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}^{*},\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\left\|\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\left\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{\top}(\mathbf{Z}-\mathbf{Z}^{*}),\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}\right\rangle+\frac{1}{2}\left\langle-\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}^{*},\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\left\|\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\left\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{\top}(\mathbf{Z}-\mathbf{Z}^{*}),\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}\right\rangle+\frac{1}{2}\left\langle\mathbf{Z}^{*\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{*}-\mathbf{Z}^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{*},\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\left\|\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\left\langle(\mathbf{Z}-\mathbf{Z}^{*})^{\top}(\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}-\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{*}),\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\left\|\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{Z}-\mathbf{Z}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\left\|\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}-\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}\left\|\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

Note that
\left\|\mathbf{Z}-\mathbf{Z}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}=\left\|\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}-\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}=\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{A}_{1}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}-\mathbf{A}_{2}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}
. By Lemma
C.3
with
c_{b}\leq 0.01
, we have
\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{A}_{1}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}-\mathbf{A}_{2}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\leq 50\phi^{4/3}\mathrm{dist}^{2}_{(j)}
, and hence,

\left\langle\mathbf{Z}-\mathbf{Z}^{*},\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}\widetilde{\mathbf{Z}}^{\top}\mathbf{Z}\right\rangle\geq\frac{1}{2}\left(\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)^{2}-25\phi^{4/3}\mathrm{dist}^{2}_{(j)}\left|\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right|.

Combining the two pieces, we have the lower bound of
G
terms:

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2}\left(G_{\mathbf{D}_{i}}+G_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}+G_{\mathbf{P}_{i}}+G_{\mathbf{C}_{i}}\right)

(80)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\left(\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)^{2}-45\phi^{4/3}\mathrm{dist}^{2}_{(j)}\left|\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right|

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\left(\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)^{2}-1200\phi^{8/3}\mathrm{dist}^{4}_{(j)}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\left(\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)^{2}-\frac{1200C_{D}}{\kappa^{2}}\phi^{10/3}\mathrm{dist}^{2}_{(j)}.

Step 3.3.
(Lower bound for
\sum_{i=1}^{2}\left(T_{\mathbf{R}_{i}}+T_{\mathbf{P}_{i}}+T_{\mathbf{C}_{i}}\right)
)

Similarly to
Wang et al., (2023)
, it can be verified that

\displaystyle T_{\mathbf{R}_{1}}+T_{\mathbf{P}_{1}}+T_{\mathbf{C}_{1}}+T_{\mathbf{R}_{2}}+T_{\mathbf{P}_{2}}+T_{\mathbf{C}_{2}}

(81)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\langle\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}\right]-\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{*}~\mathbf{R}_{1}^{*}\right]\mathbf{O}_{1,u}^{(j)},\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}\right]\left(\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}\right]^{\top}\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}\right]-b^{2}\mathbf{I}_{r_{1}}\right)\right\rangle

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\left\langle\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)}\right]-\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{*}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{*}\right]\mathbf{O}_{1,v}^{(j)},\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)}\right]\left(\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)}\right]^{\top}\left[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)}\right]-b^{2}\mathbf{I}_{r_{1}}\right)\right\rangle

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\left\langle\left[\mathbf{C}_{2}^{(j)}~\mathbf{R}_{2}^{(j)}\right]-\left[\mathbf{C}_{2}^{*}~\mathbf{R}_{2}^{*}\right]\mathbf{O}_{2,u}^{(j)},\left[\mathbf{C}_{2}^{(j)}~\mathbf{R}_{2}^{(j)}\right]\left(\left[\mathbf{C}_{2}^{(j)}~\mathbf{R}_{2}^{(j)}\right]^{\top}\left[\mathbf{C}_{2}^{(j)}~\mathbf{R}_{2}^{(j)}\right]-b^{2}\mathbf{I}_{r_{2}}\right)\right\rangle

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\left\langle\left[\mathbf{C}_{2}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{2}^{(j)}\right]-\left[\mathbf{C}_{2}^{*}~\mathbf{P}_{2}^{*}\right]\mathbf{O}_{2,v}^{(j)},\left[\mathbf{C}_{2}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{2}^{(j)}\right]\left(\left[\mathbf{C}_{2}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{2}^{(j)}\right]^{\top}\left[\mathbf{C}_{2}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{2}^{(j)}\right]-b^{2}\mathbf{I}_{r_{2}}\right)\right\rangle.

Denote
\mathbf{U}_{1}^{(j)}=[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{R}_{1}^{(j)}],\mathbf{V}_{1}^{(j)}=[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{(j)}],\mathbf{U}_{1}^{*}=[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{*}~\mathbf{R}_{1}^{*}],\mathbf{V}_{1}^{*}=[\mathbf{C}_{1}^{*}~\mathbf{P}_{1}^{*}]
. Recall that
\mathbf{U}_{1}^{*\top}\mathbf{U}_{1}^{*}=b^{2}\mathbf{I}_{r_{1}}
and
\mathbf{V}_{1}^{*\top}\mathbf{V}_{1}^{*}=b^{2}\mathbf{I}_{r_{1}}
, for the first term we have

\displaystyle\left\langle\mathbf{U}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{U}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,u}^{(j)},\mathbf{U}_{1}^{(j)}\left(\mathbf{U}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{U}_{1}^{(j)}-b^{2}\mathbf{I}_{r_{1}}\right)\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\left\langle\mathbf{U}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{U}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{U}_{1}^{*\top}\mathbf{U}_{1}^{*},\mathbf{U}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{U}_{1}^{(j)}-b^{2}\mathbf{I}_{r_{1}}\right\rangle+\frac{1}{2}\left\langle\mathbf{U}_{1}^{(j)\top}(\mathbf{U}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{U}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,u}^{(j)}),\mathbf{U}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{U}_{1}^{(j)}-b^{2}\mathbf{I}_{r_{1}}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\left\langle\mathbf{U}_{1}^{*\top}\mathbf{U}_{1}^{*}-\mathbf{U}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{U}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,u}^{(j)},\mathbf{U}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{U}_{1}^{(j)}-b^{2}\mathbf{I}_{r_{1}}\right\rangle.

Since
\mathbf{U}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{U}_{1}^{(j)}-b^{2}\mathbf{I}_{r_{1}}
is symmetric, we have

\displaystyle\left\langle\mathbf{U}_{1}^{*\top}\mathbf{U}_{1}^{*}-\mathbf{U}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{U}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,u}^{(j)},\mathbf{U}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{U}_{1}^{(j)}-b^{2}\mathbf{I}_{r_{1}}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\langle\mathbf{U}_{1}^{*\top}\mathbf{U}_{1}^{*}-\mathbf{O}_{1,u}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{U}_{1}^{*\top}\mathbf{U}_{1}^{(j)},\mathbf{U}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{U}_{1}^{(j)}-b^{2}\mathbf{I}_{r_{1}}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\langle\mathbf{O}_{1,u}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{U}_{1}^{*\top}(\mathbf{U}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,u}^{(j)}-\mathbf{U}_{1}^{(j)}),\mathbf{U}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{U}_{1}^{(j)}-b^{2}\mathbf{I}_{r_{1}}\right\rangle.

Therefore, with condition (
41
),

\displaystyle\left\langle\mathbf{U}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{U}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,u}^{(j)},\mathbf{U}_{1}^{(j)}\left(\mathbf{U}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{U}_{1}^{(j)}-b^{2}\mathbf{I}_{r_{1}}\right)\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{U}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{U}_{1}^{(j)}-b^{2}\mathbf{I}_{r_{1}}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\left\langle(\mathbf{U}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{U}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,u}^{(j)})^{\top}(\mathbf{U}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{U}_{1}^{*}\mathbf{O}_{1,u}^{(j)}),\mathbf{U}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{U}_{1}^{(j)}-b^{2}\mathbf{I}_{r_{1}}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{U}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{U}_{1}^{(j)}-b^{2}\mathbf{I}_{r_{1}}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\mathrm{dist}^{2}_{(j)}\left\|\mathbf{U}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{U}_{1}^{(j)}-b^{2}\mathbf{I}_{r_{1}}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\left\|\mathbf{U}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{U}_{1}^{(j)}-b^{2}\mathbf{I}_{r_{1}}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\frac{1}{4}\mathrm{dist}^{4}_{(j)}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\left\|\mathbf{U}_{1}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{U}_{1}^{(j)}-b^{2}\mathbf{I}_{r_{1}}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\frac{C_{D}}{4}\phi^{2/3}\mathrm{dist}^{2}_{(j)}.

Adding the lower bounds of the four terms in (
81
) together, we have

\displaystyle T_{\mathbf{R}_{1}}+T_{\mathbf{P}_{1}}+T_{\mathbf{C}_{1}}+T_{\mathbf{R}_{2}}+T_{\mathbf{P}_{2}}+T_{\mathbf{C}_{2}}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\left(\left\|\left[\mathbf{C}_{i}^{(j)}~\mathbf{R}_{i}^{(j)}\right]^{\top}\left[\mathbf{C}_{i}^{(j)}~\mathbf{R}_{i}^{(j)}\right]-b^{2}\mathbf{I}_{r_{i}}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\left\|\left[\mathbf{C}_{i}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{i}^{(j)}\right]^{\top}\left[\mathbf{C}_{i}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{i}^{(j)}\right]-b^{2}\mathbf{I}_{r_{i}}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle C_{D}\phi^{2/3}\mathrm{dist}^{2}_{(j)}.

Step 3.4.
(Upper bound for
\sum_{i=1}^{2}\left(Q_{\mathbf{D}_{i},2}+Q_{\mathbf{R}_{i},2}+Q_{\mathbf{P}_{i},2}+Q_{\mathbf{C}_{i},2}\right)
)

Following the definitions and plugging in
b=\phi^{1/3}
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2}\left(Q_{\mathbf{D}_{i},2}+Q_{\mathbf{R}_{i},2}+Q_{\mathbf{P}_{i},2}+Q_{\mathbf{C}_{i},2}\right)

(82)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 232\phi^{10/3}\left(\xi^{2}(r_{1},r_{2},d_{1},d_{2})+\left\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\lambda_{2}^{2}\phi^{2/3}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\left(18\left\|\mathbf{C}_{i}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{R}_{i}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+18\left\|\mathbf{C}_{i}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{P}_{i}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+40\left\|\mathbf{C}_{i}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{C}_{i}-b^{2}\mathbf{I}_{d_{i}}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right.

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\left.8\left\|\mathbf{R}_{i}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{R}_{i}-b^{2}\mathbf{I}_{r_{i}-d_{i}}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+8\left\|\mathbf{P}_{i}^{(j)\top}\mathbf{P}_{i}-b^{2}\mathbf{I}_{r_{i}-d_{i}}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle 120\lambda_{1}^{2}\phi^{10/3}\left(\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 232\phi^{10/3}\left(\xi^{2}(r_{1},r_{2},d_{1},d_{2})+\left\|\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{(j)})-\nabla\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}(\mathbf{A}^{*})\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle 20\lambda_{2}^{2}\phi^{2/3}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\left(\left\|\left[\mathbf{C}_{i}^{(j)}~\mathbf{R}_{i}^{(j)}\right]^{\top}\left[\mathbf{C}_{i}^{(j)}~\mathbf{R}_{i}^{(j)}\right]-b^{2}\mathbf{I}_{r_{i}}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}+\left\|\left[\mathbf{C}_{i}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{i}^{(j)}\right]^{\top}\left[\mathbf{C}_{i}^{(j)}~\mathbf{P}_{i}^{(j)}\right]-b^{2}\mathbf{I}_{r_{i}}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle 120\lambda_{1}^{2}\phi^{10/3}\left(\left\|\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}\right)^{2}.

Step 3.5.
(Lower bound for
\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\otimes\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{A}_{2}^{*}\otimes\mathbf{A}_{1}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}
)

So far, we have derived bounds for all parts in (
68
). Combining these pieces, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{dist}^{2}_{(j+1)}-\mathrm{dist}^{2}_{(j)}

(83)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\alpha\eta\left\|\mathbf{A}_{2}^{(j)}\otimes\mathbf{A}_{1}^{(j)}-\mathbf{A}_{2}^{*}\otimes\mathbf{A}_{1}^{*}\right\|_{\mathrm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle-
